# gpu-tensor route — IR after each pass (heat2d)

Generated by `stencil-dsl/scripts/dump_pass_ir.py` from the real compiler output (`stencil-opt --mlir-print-ir-after-all`). A pass whose output is the same as the previous pass's is not printed; the stage index lists every pass. Grid 1024 x 1024, 100 steps. Blocks over 120 lines are cut in the middle (marked). The tile and block sizes shown are those the compiler chooses for this grid; they differ for other sizes. Chip `sm_90`, `fp-mode=relaxed` (matrix units regroup the arithmetic).
